### [1] [Discrete time liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30266)
*Zuo Wang,Paul M. Schindler,Marin Bukov,Marios H. Michael*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文首次提出并识别"离散时间液体"这一新型时空序，它由时间孤子（time-solitons）导致的短程时间相干性所表征。在一个与有限温度腔耦合的二维自旋系统中，自旋形成周期倍增的亚稳态，但单个轨迹会在两种对称相关的时间构型间随机切换，这些切换即时间孤子——它们保持局域周期倍增却破坏长程时间相干性。作者提出最小模型复现了多体系统的时间关联分布，指出时间液体可视为含有限密度时间孤子的时间晶体，并因其孤子可在任意时刻出现，系综平均恢复连续时间平移对称性，从而暗示离散时间液体可能在热平衡中存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自从离散时间晶体被发现以来，一个核心问题是：除了时间晶体所代表的时间序之外，是否还存在其他类型的时序多体序。作者旨在探索在驱动相干性有限、且系统处于非平衡但有噪声扰动的条件下，能否涌现出新的时空序形态，从而扩展对周期驱动系统中自发对称破缺与拓扑缺陷的理解。

Method: 作者研究一个与有限温度腔耦合的二维自旋系统，利用腔的涨落提供带噪、仅具有限时间相干性的近似周期驱动。通过数值模拟观察自旋形成的周期倍增亚稳态，追踪单个轨迹中两种时间构型间的随机切换，并将这些切换识别为拓扑缺陷——时间孤子。进一步，作者构建一个最小模型，用于复现多体系统的时间关联函数分布，从而抽象出时间液体的普适描述。

Result: 在较宽的参数范围内，自旋系统尽管驱动源时间相干性有限，仍能形成稳健的周期倍增亚稳态。与时间晶体不同，该态仅具有限时间相干性：轨迹会在两种对称相关的时间构型间随机切换。这些切换被证实为时间孤子——它们保持局域周期倍增，却破坏长程时空相干性。最小模型成功复现了多体系统的时间关联分布。由于时间孤子可在任意时刻出现，系综平均恢复了连续时间平移对称性，暗示离散时间液体可能存在于热平衡中。

Conclusion: 作者识别出离散时间液体这一新型时空序，其本质是携有有限密度时间孤子的时间晶体。时间孤子作为时间序中的拓扑缺陷，在维持局域周期倍增的同时破坏了长程时间相干性。这一发现将时间晶体概念推广到有限相干性情形，并提出了离散时间液体可能在热平衡中存在的可能性，为理解周期驱动多体系统中的时空序与拓扑缺陷提供了新框架。

Abstract: Discrete time crystals exhibit robust subharmonic oscillations, spontaneously breaking the discrete time-translation symmetry of a periodic drive. Since their emergence, the question of whether other types of temporal many-body order can exist has attracted considerable attention. Here we identify discrete time liquids -- a new form of spatiotemporal order characterized by short-range temporal coherence as a result of time-solitons. We study a two-dimensional spin system coupled to a finite-temperature cavity, whose fluctuations provide a noisy, approximately periodic drive with only finite temporal coherence. Remarkably, the spins nevertheless form a metastable state with robust period-doubled dynamics over an extended parameter regime. Unlike a time crystal, this state has only finite temporal coherence: individual trajectories stochastically switch between the two symmetry-related temporal configurations of the period-doubled response. We identify these switches as time-solitons: topological defects in temporal order that preserve local period doubling while destroying long-range temporal coherence, thereby producing the time liquid state. We propose a minimal model which reproduces the distribution of temporal correlations of the many-body system, suggesting a general description of the time liquid as a time crystal with a finite density of time-solitons. Because these solitons can occur at arbitrary times, ensemble averaging restores continuous time-translation symmetry, raising the intriguing possibility that discrete time liquids can exist in thermal equilibrium.

</details>


### [2] [Nonlinear Canonical Transformations for Studying Spin-1/2 Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30403)
*Swarnamoy Ghosh,Thomas M. Henderson,Gustavo E. Scuseria*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种非线性自旋-费米子-自旋（SFS）变换，将强关联自旋哈密顿量映射到一个平均场描述更准确的新自旋哈密顿量，并指出该变换可用紧凑的 Clifford 电路实现。在 XXZ 和 J1-J2 模型上的基准测试显示，变换后的平均场及自旋-BCS 耦合簇方法显著降低了能量误差，表明精确算子表示变换可作为强关联自旋系统平均场与低秩耦合簇近似的多体预条件器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联量子系统（如阻挫自旋晶格）的基态常具有强多参考特性与对称破缺，使传统耦合簇方法失效；多参考与对称投影耦合簇理论尚非黑箱方法，需系统特定的开发，而自旋-BCS 耦合簇方法在强关联区域可能需要极高激发阶数才能恢复精确的基态物理，亟需能提升平均场与低秩近似精度的通用手段。

Method: 作者引入非线性自旋-费米子-自旋（SFS）变换，把一个强关联自旋哈密顿量映射为基态在平均场层面描述更准确的新自旋哈密顿量；对代表性 SFS 变换识别出紧凑的 Clifford 电路，将变换后的乘积态映射回原表示中经电路“修饰”的、一般纠缠的态。随后对变换后的哈密顿量采用破缺对称平均场理论及后平均场方法处理，并在 XXZ 与 J1-J2 模型上进行数值基准测试。

Result: 在 XXZ 与 J1-J2 哈密顿量上的基准表明，变换表示下的自旋平均场相较传统自旋-BCS 平均场降低了能量误差；对一维 XXZ 模型，变换后的自旋-BCS 耦合簇计算相比未变换方法显著提升精度；在二维情况下，变换后平均场解的精度依赖于格点排序与边界条件，因为可能出现非局域弦（nonlocal strings）。

Conclusion: 精确的算子表示变换可以作为多体预条件器，显著改善强关联自旋系统上平均场与低秩耦合簇近似的精度，为强关联、阻挫自旋模型提供了一条更具通用性的计算路径。

Abstract: Strongly correlated quantum systems, including frustrated spin lattices, remain challenging for conventional coupled-cluster methods because their ground states often exhibit strong multireference character and symmetry breaking. Although multireference and symmetry-projected coupled-cluster theories are promising, they are not yet black-box methods and require system-specific development. The XXZ and $J_1$-$J_2$ spin models provide demanding benchmarks. Spin-BCS-based coupled-cluster methods may require high excitation ranks to recover the exact ground-state physics in strongly correlated regimes. We introduce a nonlinear spin-fermion-spin (SFS) transformation designed to map a strongly correlated spin Hamiltonian into a new spin Hamiltonian whose ground state is more accurately described at the mean-field level. For a representative SFS transformation, we identify a compact Clifford circuit that maps transformed product states to circuit-dressed, generally entangled states in the original representation. The transformed Hamiltonian can then be treated using broken-symmetry mean-field theory and post-mean-field methods. Benchmarks for the XXZ and $J_1$-$J_2$ Hamiltonians show that spin mean-field theory in the transformed representation reduces the energy error relative to conventional spin-BCS mean field. The spin-BCS-based coupled-cluster calculations for the one-dimensional XXZ model provide substantially improved accuracy relative to the corresponding untransformed methods. In 2D, the accuracy of the transformed mean-field solutions depends on the site ordering and boundary conditions, as nonlocal strings can appear. These results show that exact transformations of operator representations can serve as many-body preconditioners for mean-field and low-rank coupled-cluster approximations to strongly correlated spin systems.

</details>


### [3] [Charge Density Waves and In-Gap States in Coupled Chains and Moiré Superlattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30281)
*Muhammad Akram,Onur Erten*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究耦合的Su-Schrieffer-Heeger(SSH)链体系,包括晶格匹配的梯子结构和晶格失配的摩尔超晶格中的电荷密度波(CDW)相与对称性保护的束缚态。研究发现链间隧穿和晶格失配可作为调控一维链中CDW序和束缚态的自然控制参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电荷密度波源于电子与晶格畸变的相互作用,是低维体系的标志性现象。作者旨在探讨耦合一维SSH链中电子-声子耦合与链间隧穿、晶格失配之间的竞争如何产生丰富的CDW相及束缚态,从而揭示调控低维CDW的新途径。

Method: 作者理论研究了耦合的Su-Schrieffer-Heeger链,分为两类:(1)晶格匹配的梯子结构;(2)晶格失配形成的摩尔超晶格。通过分析电子-声子耦合与链间隧穿之间的竞争,系统地研究CDW相序、能隙结构及对称性对束缚态的保护机制。

Result: 在晶格匹配的链中,电子-声子耦合与链间隧穿的竞争驱动了一系列CDW相,包括交错二聚体、三聚体和非公度态;这些相通常有能隙,并承载由晶体对称性(而非手性对称性)保护的束缚态,因而束缚态不固定在零能。晶格失配进一步将这些相重构为长周期的"超摩尔"CDW,其中包含有序排列的缺陷,缺陷被钉扎在摩尔长度尺度上并局域化电子态。

Conclusion: 链间隧穿和晶格失配可作为天然的控制参数,用于调节一维耦合链中的CDW序和束缚态;晶体对称性保护的束缚态不需要手性对称性,且可偏离零能,这为理解和设计低维CDW体系提供了新视角。

Abstract: Charge density waves (CDWs) arise from the interplay between electrons and lattice distortions and are a hallmark of low-dimensional systems. Here we study coupled Su-Schrieffer-Heeger chains, both lattice-matched ladders and lattice-mismatched moiré superlattices, and find a rich set of CDW phases together with symmetry-protected bound states. In lattice-matched chains, the competition between electron-phonon coupling and interchain tunneling drives a sequence of CDW phases, including staggered dimers, trimers, and incommensurate states. These phases are generally gapped and host bound states protected by crystalline symmetries rather than chiral symmetry, and are therefore not pinned to zero energy. Lattice mismatch further reconstructs these phases into long-period supermoiré CDWs, with an ordered array of defects, pinned to the moiré length scale, that host localized electronic states. Interchain tunneling and lattice mismatch thus emerge as natural control parameters for CDW order and bound states in one-dimensional chains.

</details>


### [4] [$π$-Flux Tuning of Quantum Paramagnetism in the Triangular-Lattice Hubbard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30290)
*Zhaochen Liu,Andreas Feuerpfeil,Ronny Thomale*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文比较了半填充下两种时间反演对称的三角晶格Hubbard模型（零磁通模型与交错π磁通模型），发现二者在强耦合极限下虽退化为同一海森堡模型，但因电荷涨落差异导致不同的量子顺磁相；环交换符号可作为区分手性与非手性量子顺磁态的判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两种三角晶格Hubbard模型在强耦合领头阶给出相同的海森堡模型，但其电荷涨落特征不同，因而可能进入不同的量子顺磁态。作者希望揭示如何利用环交换的符号来区分手性自旋液体与无手性序的量子顺磁相。

Method: 将强耦合展开与无限密度矩阵重整化群（iDMRG）数值计算相结合，对比半填充下零磁通模型与交错π磁通模型的环交换项及标量手性序。

Result: 零磁通模型的正环交换促进自发手性，与其中间耦合下可能的手性自旋液体图景一致；交错π磁通模型（其非相互作用极限为狄拉克半金属）的环交换改变符号、不利于手性序，且未观测到可分辨的标量手性。

Conclusion: 磁通调控的环交换是有限耦合下区分手性与非手性量子顺磁体的有效判别手段。

Abstract: We compare two time-reversal-symmetric triangular-lattice Hubbard models at half filling: the zero-flux model and a staggered $π$-flux model with zero and $π$ flux through oppositely oriented triangles. Although both reduce to the same Heisenberg model at leading order in the strong-coupling limit, their distinct charge fluctuation profiles yield different quantum paramagnetic regimes. Combining a strong-coupling expansion with infinite density matrix renormalization group (iDMRG) calculations, we find that the positive ring-exchange of the zero-flux model promotes spontaneous chirality, consistent with its notion of a chiral spin liquid at intermediate coupling. In the staggered-$π$-flux model, whose noninteracting limit is a Dirac semimetal, the ring exchange changes sign and disfavors chiral order and we find no resolvable scalar chirality. This renders flux-tuned ring-exchange an efficient discriminator between chiral and non-chiral quantum paramagnets at finite coupling.

</details>


### [5] [Extreme competition between stripes and superconductivity in the hole-doped Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30321)
*Ao Chen,Johnnie Gray,Si-Jing Du,Garnet Kin-Lic Chan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文使用神经张量网络和神经反流态两类先进变分方法，在多达1024个格点的Hubbard模型上取得了前所未有的变分精度，发现在1/8掺杂下均匀d波态、超导条纹态与非超导条纹态之间存在毫t量级的近简并，揭示了条纹与超导之间的极端竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维Hubbard模型最初被提出作为铜氧化物高温超导体中均匀d波超导的最小模型，但近期在现实掺杂区间的计算却常发现其他基态；同时不同计算方法存在固有偏差，导致结果存在不确定性。作者旨在通过高精度、可互相印证的计算方法消除这种不确定性。

Method: 采用先进的神经张量网络（neural tensor network）和神经反流态（neural backflow state）变分模拟，借助大规模GPU计算，在最多1024个格点的晶格上达到前所未有的变分精度；分别研究纯Hubbard模型及加入次近邻跃迁（与空穴掺杂铜氧化物相关）的情形，聚焦1/8掺杂。

Result: 在1/8掺杂的纯Hubbard模型及含次近邻跃迁的模型中，低能均匀d波态、超导条纹与非超导条纹态之间呈现毫t（milli-$t$）能量尺度的近简并，表明不存在单一主导序。

Conclusion: 该工作重新平衡了“Hubbard模型在相关参数区间支持单一主导序”的传统叙事，转而描绘出条纹与超导之间极端竞争的图景。

Abstract: The two-dimensional Hubbard model was originally proposed as the minimal model of uniform $d$-wave superconductivity in the cuprate high-temperature superconductors, but recent calculations with relevant parameters in a realistic doping regime have often found other ground states. Further, uncertainty remains over such results due to the intrinsic bias of different computational methods. Here we address this uncertainty through state-of-the-art neural tensor network and neural backflow state simulations, enabled by large-scale GPU computation, that together reach an unprecedented variational accuracy on lattices with up to 1024 sites. At $1/8$ doping in the pure Hubbard model, as well as with next-nearest-neighbor hopping relevant to the hole-doped cuprates, we reveal a near degeneracy among a low-energy uniform $d$-wave state, superconducting stripes, and non-superconducting stripe states on the milli-$t$ energy scale. Our work rebalances the narrative that the Hubbard model supports a single dominant order in relevant parameter regimes towards a picture of extreme competition between stripes and superconductivity.

</details>


### [6] [Emergent altermagnetism in (LaVO$_3$)$_n$/(LaNiO$_3$)$_m$ superlattices from interfacial charge transfer and octahedral tilts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30371)
*Gustavo H. Cassemiro,Walber H. Brito*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文通过第一性原理计算证明，在 (LaVO3)n/(LaNiO3)m 超晶格中，界面电荷转移与八面体倾斜的协同作用可涌现出交变磁性（altermagnetism），并产生无需自旋轨道耦合的 d 波自旋劈裂，其中 (1,1) 绝缘超晶格劈裂达 118 meV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在氧化物异质结构中通过界面工程实现交变磁性的普适设计原理。此前研究多关注块体材料（如 LaVO3）中的交变磁性，块体劈裂值有限；作者希望利用界面电荷转移这一新机制，将原本顺磁的镍酸盐层激活为磁性亚晶格，从而获得更强的自旋劈裂并拓展材料体系。

Method: 采用基于密度泛函理论的 DFT+U 第一性原理计算，系统研究 (LaVO3)n/(LaNiO3)m 超晶格（(n,m)=(1,1)、(2,2)、(2,4)）。通过分析界面电荷转移诱导的价态重构（V 趋向 +4 价、Ni 趋向 +2 价）、C 型反铁磁序与 a⁻b⁺c⁻ 或 a⁻b⁻c⁻ 八面体倾斜的结合，确认相反自旋亚晶格仅通过非简单空间群对称操作联系，进而计算并分解 d 波自旋劈裂的能带来源（V、Ni 的 3d 轨道及 O 的 2p 轨道权重）。

Result: 界面电荷转移将体系驱动至 V⁴⁺/Ni²⁺ 构型，使顺磁的镍酸盐层变为磁性亚晶格。C 型反铁磁序与特定八面体倾斜共同作用，使相反自旋亚晶格仅由非简单空间群对称操作关联，从而在无自旋轨道耦合下产生 d 波自旋劈裂。绝缘 (LVO)1/(LNO)1 超晶格中劈裂达 118 meV，约为块体 LaVO3 的两倍，且随镍酸盐层增厚而减小。该劈裂同时由 V 和 Ni 亚晶格承载，其中近一半权重来自氧 2p 态的 p-d 共价杂化。

Conclusion: 界面电荷转移可作为在氧化物异质结构中工程设计交变磁性的有效原理，通过激活原本顺磁的镍酸盐层并借助 p-d 共价显著增强 d 波自旋劈裂，为开发无自旋轨道耦合的自旋电子学材料提供了新途径。

Abstract: Using first-principles calculations, we show that altermagnetism emerges in (LaVO$_3$)$_n$/(LaNiO$_3$)$_m$ superlattices, with $(n,m) = (1,1), (2,2)$ and $(2,4)$, from the interplay of interfacial charge transfer and octahedral tilts. Charge transfer drives the system towards the V$^{4+}$ and Ni$^{2+}$ configuration, turning the otherwise paramagnetic nickelate layers into a magnetically active sublattice. The resulting $C$-type antiferromagnetic order, combined with $a^-b^+c^-$ or $a^-b^-c^-$ octahedral tilts, links opposite-spin sublattices only through nonsymmorphic operations, yielding a $d$-wave spin splitting without spin-orbit coupling. Within DFT+U, the splitting reaches 118 meV in the insulating (LVO)$_1$/(LNO)$_1$ superlattice, about twice that calculated for bulk LaVO$_3$, and decreases as the nickelate layers become thicker. Our findings indicate that this splitting is hosted by both the V and Ni sublattices, with nearly half of its weight on oxygen $2p$ states through $p$-$d$ covalency. Interfacial charge transfer thus emerges as a \textit{design} principle for engineering altermagnetism in oxide heterostructures.

</details>


### [7] [Anisotropic thermoelectric transport in the Sr2RuO4-Ru eutectic system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30547)
*Takeru Ito,Shogo Yoshida,Hiroshi Yaguchi,Ryuji Okazaki*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究 Sr2RuO4-Ru 共晶体系中正常态的面内热电响应，发现热电势沿平行/垂直于 Ru 薄片方向存在显著各向异性，并通过电子结构输运计算和有限元应变建模证明该各向异性主要源于热膨胀失配导致的 Sr2RuO4 费米面修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sr2RuO4-Ru 共晶体系存在约 3 K 的增强超导转变温度（3-K 相），但其机制尚不明确。作者通过研究正常态面内热电势的各向异性，探究 Ru 夹杂物附近应变对 Sr2RuO4 电子结构的影响，以理解共晶界面在超导增强中的作用。

The calculations show that uniaxial compression of Sr2RuO4 produces a sizable thermopower anisotropy through modification of the Fermi surfaces. When an idealized spatially inhomogeneous strain field around the Ru lamellae is included, the observed sign and qualitative magnitude of the anisotropy are reproduced more closely than in a simple unstrained two-component model. These results are consistent with a substantial contribution from strain-induced changes in the Sr2RuO4 electronic structure near the interfaces, although contributions from interfacial phase mixing and three-dimensional transport connectivity cannot be excluded.

We find that unrestricted BTS closely approaches optimized DOCI in the repulsive regime, substantially improves upon AGP, and reproduces DOCI density correlations more accurately than pCCD

Abstract: The intrinsic anomalous Hall effect (AHE), originating from Berry curvature, has been extensively studied in $f$-electron systems, including Ce-based compounds. Here, we investigate this issue in CeRu$_2$Si$_2$ by calculating the intrinsic anomalous Hall conductivity (AHC) for several occupied Ce-$4f$ states using first-principles LDA+$U$ calculations combined with Wannier interpolation method. We find that the intrinsic AHC remains finite even when the Ce-$4f$ states are localized and do not contribute directly to the Fermi surface, and that its magnitude depends on which Ce-$4f$ state is occupied. We show that localized $4f$-electron magnetism induces exchange splitting in the conduction bands, generating Berry curvature in specific regions near the Fermi surface. This mechanism enables conduction bands that are derived from nonmagnetic orbitals and form the Fermi surface to contribute to the intrinsic AHE, suggesting a route to realizing large intrinsic AHE in $f$-electron materials.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 在 AMEA 框架下，用适用于任意杂化函数的近似求解器（如推广到自旋极化解的非平衡 Kajueter-Kotliar 迭代微扰理论 IPT，也可用非交叉近似 NCA 等）估计物理杂化与辅助杂化自能之比，并利用该比值把 AMEA 的自能从辅助情形推广到物理情形。该修正当拟合或近似求解器任一精确时为精确。

Abstract: The auxiliary master equation approach (AMEA) is a nonperturbative impurity solver for steady-state nonequilibrium dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT). It replaces the physical hybridization function by that of a small auxiliary open quantum system, whose parameters are fitted to reproduce it, and then solves the resulting many-body problem essentially exactly. AMEA's accuracy is thus limited by the quality of this fit: since the fastest-varying part of the hybridization is set by the distribution function, whose Fermi edges sharpen at low temperature, the fit tends to overestimate the temperature, especially for weak reservoir coupling. For the auxiliary hybridization itself, however, AMEA's self-energy is essentially exact. We correct for the fit error by estimating, via an approximate solver applicable to arbitrary hybridization functions, the ratio between the self-energies of the physical and auxiliary hybridizations, and using it to extend AMEA's self-energy from the auxiliary to the physical case. This fit-error correction becomes exact whenever either the fit or the approximate solver is exact. We benchmark the scheme using the nonequilibrium Kajueter-Kotliar iterated perturbation theory (IPT), generalized here to spin-polarized solutions, as the approximate solver; other approximate solvers, such as the noncrossing approximation, can be used as well. Applied to an Anderson impurity model with a spin-polarized bath, and within DMFT to a Hubbard layer weakly coupled to two metallic reservoirs, the correction largely removes the spurious heating from the AMEA fit and from insufficient thermalization by the reservoirs, while preserving the correct description of the Mott insulator, which IPT alone cannot fully recover.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate magnetic correlations in the two-leg Kondo-Hubbard ladder using the density-matrix renormalization group method. We focus on a parameter regime in which the noninteracting electronic system has a narrow indirect band gap. In this regime, there is no Fermi surface, and the conventional Fermi-surface-based Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yosida picture is not directly applicable; magnetic correlations between localized spins become weaker as the noninteracting band gap increases. We find that a moderate on-site Coulomb interaction enhances the antiferromagnetic correlations between localized spins. This enhancement, which is also seen in the staggered magnetization induced by a pinning field at the edges, occurs for both ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic Kondo couplings. Our results show that electron-electron interactions can enhance magnetic correlations between localized spins even when the underlying noninteracting electronic structure is gapped, suggesting an interaction-driven route to magnetic instabilities in narrow-gap Kondo-lattice systems.

</details>

Abstract: Collective ordering in correlated electron systems emerges from microscopic dynamics, yet the evolution of observable textures need not admit a closed, memoryless description. We introduce a graph-recurrent neural network (GRNN) framework that learns such emergent dynamics directly from trajectories without specifying microscopic equations of motion. The framework integrates gated recurrent memory with graph message passing: local hidden states encode information beyond the instantaneous observables, while spatial coupling allows this information to propagate between sites. The observable and hidden fields evolve jointly through a shared local rule, providing a scalable representation of dynamics with spatial interactions and temporal memory. We demonstrate this framework on the emergent dynamics of charge-density-wave order in the semiclassical Holstein model, successfully reproducing temporal and spatial correlations and dynamical scaling during domain coarsening. By learning finite-time evolution directly, the GRNN offers a route to efficient, data-driven descriptions of collective dynamics with unresolved microscopic degrees of freedom.

</details>

Abstract: The sign problem poses a fundamental barrier to numerically exact quantum Monte Carlo simulations, severely hindering our understanding of quantum many-body physics across disciplines ranging from condensed matter to particle and nuclear physics. Geometrical frustration makes this barrier especially acute for interacting bosons. Here, we develop a bosonic auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo (BAFQMC) method and rigorously prove that time-reversal symmetry and reflection positivity each provide a general sufficient condition for sign-problem-free simulations. Crucially, under either criterion, BAFQMC evades the sign problem even on frustrated lattices where conventional world-line and stochastic-series-expansion methods fail. Benchmarking our approach on a two-flavor Bose-Hubbard model on the triangular lattice yields excellent quantitative agreement with finite-temperature exact diagonalization. By establishing unifying principles to solve the sign problem in BAFQMC, our work substantially expands the reach of unbiased, numerically exact simulations for frustrated bosonic matter.

</details>

Motivation: 施主掺杂可通过改变晶格畸变和电子屏蔽来调控钙钛矿氧化物的铁电、介电、电子和光学性质，但其微观机制尚需系统阐明。作者选择La掺杂BaTiO3作为模型体系，探究施主诱导的屏蔽效应对极化和相关性质的影响。

For a model with 12.5% La substitution at the Ba site, La incorporation induces anisotropic lattice contraction, reduces tetragonality and Ti off-centering, and weakens the energetic stabilization of the tetragonal ferroelectric phase. The accompanying reduction in polarization is associated with enhanced electronic screening and reduced anomalous Born effective charges. Phonon calculations indicate that the relaxed doped structure is dynamically stable, while hardening of a low-frequency polar mode is consistent with weakened ferroelectricity. The high-frequency dielectric response increases moderately, whereas the static dielectric response decreases as the lattice contribution is suppressed. Electronic-structure calculations show a Fermi-level shift toward the conduction band without mid-gap defect states. The optical spectra exhibit an absorption-edge shift consistent with a Burstein-Moss-type mechanism, enhanced near-edge transparency, and reduced birefringence. Together, these results suggest a microscopic connection between donor-induced screening, polar-mode hardening, reduced dynamical charges, polarization suppression, and decreased optical anisotropy. Here, we review its origin, its links to hydrodynamic Euler equations and to the fundamental paradox surrounding the parabolic heat equation and its fate across the quantum-classical crossover.

Specifically, we study damage induced by cyclic electron beam (e-beam) exposure under different combinations of base temperature and heat flux. We carry out component-scale thermo-mechanical simulations using CabanaPD, a peridynamics fracture mechanics code, informed by experimental data for recrystallized pure tungsten from the JUDITH e-beam facility. The simulations compare well with the experimental classification and enable identification of cracking thresholds across a range of heat fluxes and base temperatures. These predictions can provide detailed insights into the spatial distribution and temporal accumulation of cracks and damage, while offering a pathway for screening candidate PFMs and quantifying their lifespan under fusion-relevant conditions.

Abstract: Metal additive manufacturing (AM) offers substantial advantages to the fabrication of complex structural components, but its widespread adoption is slowed due to complexities in the process-structure-property relations which is the cause for uncertainty and drives risk. Accurately quantifying these complex relations solely via experimental workflows is time consuming and costly. Integrated computational materials and engineering (ICME) frameworks address this challenge by leveraging advanced computational tools, such as crystal plasticity finite element models (CPFEM), to predict critical mechanical properties directly from microstructural data. However, these frameworks require a large datasets that are often difficult to obtain, and their predictive accuracy depends on the availability of realistic AM microstructure representations. Generative deep learning models are a promising approach towards synthesizing AM microstructures which contain complex morphological and crystallographic features. Denoising diffusion probabilistic models (DDPMs), in particular, show a strong potential to capture the inherent complexities of AM structures. In this work, we demonstrate that DDPMs can reliably synthesize digital-twin orientation-imaging micrographs, and we support this claim with quantitative evidence of the fidelity of the generated samples. Additionally, we establish a classifier-free guidance framework for solving complex inverse problems such as property-to-structure and process-to-structure mappings, and we showcase a potential application on a qualification-relevant uncertainty quantification problem. Our work highlights the potential of AI-based generative modeling as a key component of computational frameworks for accelerating AM materials qualification and certification.

Abstract: The scattering length density (SLD) profile of a sample can be deliberately designed to enhance the information of interest obtained from neutron reflectometry. Here we show that boron segregates to an internal nitride--matrix interface in a Ni/Co-base $γ$/$γ^{\prime}$ strengthened superalloy. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层状钠过渡金属氧化物是极具前景的钠离子正极材料，但其 Na/空位有序、层堆叠序列及协同 Jahn–Teller 畸变仅在少数单一过渡金属化合物中被了解，缺乏对多过渡金属混合体系（尤其是双元 TM 氧化物）的系统性认识，限制了对相变竞争机制和材料设计的理解。

Method: 作者将已有文献报道的 Na/空位有序整理为 41 种唯一有序的库，统一应用于 5 种单一过渡金属氧化物和全部 10 种 NaxTM0.5TM'0.5O2 双元氧化物（TM = Ti, Mn, Fe, Co, Ni）。先以机器学习原子间势筛选超过 120,000 个结构，再对 1,025 个结构进行高精度第一性原理计算。通过对每个 TMO6 八面体做 Van Vleck 分析，并绘制能量密度–应变图来总结规律。

Result: 所建立的相图解释了观察到的 O3/P3/O1 序列与电压；O3 与 P3 的竞争源于每原子仅几 meV 的有序化能量差异，这是阳离子势和层间板距等标量描述符无法捕捉的。氧化还原电对形成几乎与配对金属无关的电势阶梯：Ti3+/4+ < Mn3+/4+ < Co3+/4+ < Ni2+/3+ < Fe3+/4+，解释了原始电荷转移与氧化顺序。Van Vleck 分析表明，过渡金属混合通过向 Jahn–Teller 非活性态转移电荷或稀释 Jahn–Teller 活性中心，抑制了 Mn3+ 和 Ni3+ 的协同 Jahn–Teller 畸变。能量密度–应变图给出设计规则：需要高电压电对且无 Jahn–Teller 应变，Mn4+/Ni2+ 电荷转移在 NaxMn0.5Ni0.5O2（最实用的组成）中实现了这一目标。

This is because the formation of metallic and oxide solid solutions alters the chemical activity of both the reduced metallic phases and the unreduced oxides, coupling their equilibria in ways that cannot be deduced from the stability of the pure individual oxides. Herein, we develop a CALPHAD-based thermodynamic framework to establish the compoistion-temperature-H$_2$/H$_2$O window for complete metallization of CoCrFeMnNi-based oxide mixtures. Mn and Cr generally increase oxide stability, while Co, Fe, and Ni decrease it, allowing reduction at lower H$_2$/H$_2$O ratios. The effect of each element is not fixed; it depends on the starting mixed oxide composition and can reverse when the last oxide to reduce changes. The same trends were observed for the grain that underwent a transition from normal to abnormal growth. Remarkably, even before the onset of abnormal growth, this grain was surrounded by smaller matrix grains with exclusively high-angle grain boundaries, while the local particle density was slightly higher than average. These findings suggest that the same microstructural characteristics facilitate both the emergence and persistence of abnormal grain growth, although the mechanism triggering the transition to rapid growth remains unresolved.

Abstract: We compare relative Abelian BF torsion with the Alexander-graded Euler characteristic of knot Floer homology. A meridional annulus boundary condition identifies the normalized relative torsion with the Alexander polynomial. One exact prime-cyclotomic torsion value recovers all its coefficients under a sharp genus bound, compatibly with connected-sum gluing and branched-cover formulas. We compute interface contractions and obstructions to scalar comparisons, including the pairing defect of two-sector reduction for the degree-zero torus algebra. A six-generator dg resolution yields an equivalence of perfect-module categories preserving derived pairings and admits a classical one-dimensional BV-BFV realization. In the fixed nonresonant polynomial quantum BFV sector, over characteristic zero with $\hbar$ inverted, both states and Weyl observables reduce to a vacuum field; a Hochschild invariant obstructs a Morita equivalence with the degree-zero torus algebra. These results do not establish a full geometric BV-BFV/Floer equivalence.

TL;DR: 本文证明了一维情形下带半经典常数与梯度修正的尖锐 Lieb-Thirring 不等式；在周期情形还引入了新的宇称相关壳层修正项，并刻画了优化密度剖面（构成二维“庞加莱密度流形”）。由此得到 TFW 近似的显式非渐近误差界，并证明在高密度极限下 Kohn-Sham 势以最优速率 N^{-2} 收敛到逆 Thomas-Fermi 势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密度泛函理论中 Thomas–Fermi–von Weizsäcker (TFW) 动能近似的精度问题需要精确的定量刻画；已有的 Lieb-Thirring 不等式缺乏最优常数、梯度修正以及在周期系统中对壳层效应的精确描述，导致无法给出 TFW 近似的紧致非渐近误差界与逐点/势层面的收敛速率。

Result: 得到了一维尖锐 Lieb-Thirring 不等式及其优化密度的完整表征；建立了 TFW 近似的显式非渐近全局与局部误差界，该界由到庞加莱密度流形的距离乘以粒子数控制，并表明 TFW 近似在庞加莱流形上精确成立；在周期情形证明 Kohn-Sham 势到逆 Thomas-Fermi 势的收敛速率为 O(N^{-2})，且该速率是最优的。

Conclusion: 庞加莱密度流形刻画了 TFW 近似精确成立的情形，到该流形的距离是控制 TFW 近似误差及 Kohn-Sham 势与逆 TFW 势偏差的自然几何量；高密度极限下 Kohn-Sham 势以最优二阶速率收敛到逆 Thomas-Fermi 势。这些结果为一维密度泛函理论中的动能近似和势的收敛问题提供了尖锐的定量刻画。

Abstract: We first prove a sharp Lieb-Thirring inequality with semi-classical constant and gradient correction in one dimension. In the periodic setting, the corresponding inequality also includes a parity dependent shell-correction term, which appears to be new. Moreover, we also characterize the optimizers of these inequalities. In particular, the optimizing density profiles form a two-dimensional manifold, which we call here the Poincaré density manifold.

Conclusion: 粒子与反粒子的区分可归结为 Weyl-Heisenberg 群及其中心子群 U(1)v 的量子电荷本征值 ±1/ħ，这是波函数先于动力学方程的内禀属性。通过坚持 U(1)v 不变性，即可在第一次量子化的相对论框架内彻底消除负概率、负能量和 Klein 佯谬。

Abstract: We show that the concept of particles and antiparticles is related to the Weyl-Heisenberg group $W\!H_3$. Its generators are the position operators $\hat x^a$, the momentum operators $\hat p_a$, and the central element $Q_{\sf{v}}$, which defines a subgroup U(1)$_{\sf v}$ of $W\!H_3$. We show that the wave functions have a charge relative to the group U(1)$_{\sf{v}}$ equal to either $q_+=1/\hbar$ (particle $ψ_+$) or $q_-=-1/\hbar$ (antiparticle $ψ_-$). We call these eigenvalues of the operator $Q_{\sf{v}}$ quantum charges: $Q_{\sf{v}}ψ_\pm^{}=q_\pm^{}ψ_\pm^{}$. The wave functions $ψ_+$ and $ψ_-$ belong to the Hilbert spaces $\mathcal{H}_+$ and $\mathcal{H}_-$, on which unitarily inequivalent representations $π_\hbar$ and $π_{-\hbar}$ of the Weyl-Heisenberg group $W\!H_3$ are defined. These representations are related to each other by the antilinear charge conjugation operator $\hat C$, which maps $\mathcal{H}_+, q_+$ to $\mathcal{H}_-, q_-$. Charges $q_\pm^{}$ are intrinsic characteristics of the wave functions $ψ_\pm^{}$, similar to spin and electric charge. The wave function possesses one of these charges, $q_+$ or $q_-$, prior to the specification of any equations. We show that negative probabilities/energies and the Klein paradox in Klein-Gordon and Dirac theories are consequences of the violation of U(1)$_{\sf v}$-invariance in these theories. Restoring this invariance separates particle and antiparticles and eliminates all paradoxes at the level of first-quantized relativistic theories.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 条纹畴铁磁覆盖层为 NbN 基异质结构中超导非互易性和磁通流稳定性提供了一条可重构的调控途径。

Abstract: We demonstrate reconfigurable, field-free superconducting diode behavior in NbN/FePt and NbN/AlN/FePt heterostructures with remanent stripe domains in the FePt layer. NbN films 10 nm thick are capped either directly with FePt or through a thin AlN spacer, which reduces electronic proximity effects while preserving magnetic coupling through stray fields. At zero applied magnetic field, the superconducting diode efficiency reaches approximately 50--60% in NbN/FePt when the stripes are perpendicular to the current and is strongly reduced when they are parallel. The diode effect persists in NbN/AlN/FePt with efficiencies reaching approximately 35--40%, supporting a magnetic origin of the nonreciprocity. We interpret the response as asymmetric vortex transport in the remanent magnetic landscape, with preferential edge vorticity and a Lorentz force that favors inward flux injection or outward motion depending on current polarity. Under applied perpendicular magnetic fields, the interplay between the external field and the magnetic stray fields modifies the flux-flow instability and the apparent instability velocity inferred from transport, with values below those reported for bare NbN films. These results show that stripe-domain ferromagnetic overlayers provide a reconfigurable route to control superconducting nonreciprocity and flux-flow stability in NbN-based heterostructures.

Using the MPS-based Lanczos method, we show that these localized holes give rise to in-gap bound states within the charge-transfer gap. When tunneling matrix-element effects are included, the bound states exhibit a characteristic $4a_0 \times 2a_0$ internal structure, consistent with experimental observations. TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于正则化Hartree–Fock的“源–返回”（source–return）构造，通过单次有效返回从HF信息载体直接确定标量关联能，并在Be-like序列及十电子分子、Ne-like离子上实现参数无关的定量精度，从而验证了“源强度”与“返回限制”之间的物理分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静止多电子本征态只有一个动力学相位，但其内部响应可为强多模。作者追问：静止Hartree–Fock信息载体是否足以通过一次有效返回来确定标量关联能？即关联能能否由电子间接触（contact）、维里完备（virial completion）与最小信息相对坐标模式唯一决定。

Method: 在正则化Hartree–Fock框架下，高斯原函乘积先固定一个最小信息相对坐标模式，再进行单模约化；由维里完备定义关联“源”，由精确接触度量邻接占据轨道对带来的额外“返回”限制。Be-like 1s²2s²序列确立单价电子对边界：无邻接占据价电子对时，源通过普适底值 D₀=√(1+δ²) 返回。随后用十电子分子与Ne-like离子检验有限对接触如何修饰该返回。

Result: 同一套无参数“源–返回”构造在Be-like序列、十电子分子和Ne-like离子上均复现了已知的分子与离子关联能趋势，并达到定量精度。

Conclusion: 数值一致性验证了“源强度”与“返回限制”之间物理分离的正确性，而数值精度本身并非最终目标。

Abstract: A stationary many-electron eigenstate has one dynamical phase although its internal response can be strongly multimode. We ask whether the stationary Hartree--Fock information carrier contains enough information to determine the scalar correlation energy through one effective return. In regularized Hartree--Fock, Gaussian primitive products fix a minimum-information relative-coordinate mode before any one-mode reduction; virial completion defines the correlation source, while exact contact measures the additional return restriction created by neighboring occupied pairs. The Be-like $1s^22s^2$ sequence establishes the one-valence-pair boundary: with no neighboring occupied valence pair, the source returns through the universal floor $D_0=\sqrt{1+δ^2}$. Ten-electron molecules and Ne-like ions then test how finite-pair contact dresses that return. The same parameter-free source--return construction reproduces the established molecular and ionic correlation trends with quantitative accuracy. The numerical agreement therefore serves as validation of a physical separation between source strength and return restriction, rather than as the objective by itself.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出一种由热激发占据数驱动的相变机制：当温度升高使能隙体系中的激发数目达到临界值时，体系在热力学上失稳并在两个态之间转变，表现为比热发散，但无需序参量或能隙完全闭合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多能隙体系在升温时会产生越来越多的热激发，激发间的相互作用会反过来改变能隙大小（类似受阻尼影响的谐振子频率）。作者旨在揭示这种激发占据数决定的能隙变化如何导致一种新的热力学不稳定与相变，从而解释缺乏传统序参量的转变。

Method: 作者建立一个简单的两能级模型，令能隙大小依赖于激发占据数（类似阻尼改变频率），分析激发数目随温度增长直至临界值的动力学与热力学行为；随后将该框架推广到超流^4He 和 URu2Si2，与实验观测进行对照论证。

Result: 当激发数目达到临界值时，激发占据在热力学上失稳，体系在两个态之间转变；该转变以比热发散为标志，且不要求出现序参量，也不要求能隙完全闭合。两能级系统与超流^4He 可作为示例说明，URu2Si2 的隐藏序转变被论证为这种无序参量的占据驱动转变。

Conclusion: 存在一类由热激发占据数驱动的相变，其特征是比热发散而无需序参量或能隙闭合；该机制可为 URu2Si2 的隐藏序等缺乏传统序参量的转变提供统一的理论解释。

Abstract: Systems characterized by an energy gap between the ground state and excited states support a growing number of thermal excitations upon raising the temperature. Provided these excitations interact with each other, their number affects the size of the energy gap akin to a harmonic oscillator where the oscillation frequency is damping dependent. Here we show that once a critical number of excitations is reached, the excitation population becomes thermodynamically unstable and the system transitions between two states. This transition is marked by a divergence in the specific heat, which does not have to be accompanied by an order parameter, nor by the full closure of the gap. We illustrate the thermodynamics using a simple two level system and superfluid $^4$He as examples, and we argue that the hidden order transition in URu$_2$Si$_2$ is an example of such an occupancy driven transition in the absence of an order parameter.

</details>

Conclusion: 非微扰的带隙内环态是强无序摧毁陈绝缘体陈相的微观自由度，提供了一条从微观态出发理解强无序拓扑相变的清晰路径。

Abstract: We develop a microscopic description of the strong-disorder transition in a Chern insulator using the in-gap ring states bound to individual vacancies. Using Green's function formalism, we construct an energy-dependent Hamiltonian describing their hybridization. In a commensurate periodic vacancy lattice, a Dirac inversion of the zero-energy vacancy kernel accounts for the change in the occupied-state Chern number of the full system. We treat weak positional disorder using the self-consistent Born approximation and numerical disorder averaging, and study independently distributed vacancies using transfer-matrix and multifractal scaling. The local Chern marker and current textures track the transition in real space. Together, these results highlight the role that nonperturbative ring states have as microscopic degrees of freedom through which strong disorder destroys the Chern phase.

</details>

Result: 系统相位由测量与粒子隧穿的竞争控制；该体系的体积律相总是"电荷模糊"（charge fuzzy）的；两相的相边界由 Luttinger 参数对测量率的普适依赖给出，区别于一般高激发一维系统中非普适的相边界。

Conclusion: 在玻色化框架内，无粒子数守恒的 Luttinger 液体中测量诱导相变的相边界具有普适性，其由 Luttinger 参数与测量率的关系决定，与超出 Luttinger 液体描述的高激发一维系统的非普适行为形成对照。

Abstract: We discuss phases of interacting fermionic Luttinger liquids without particle number conservation, such as those in proximity to a superconductor, subjected to weak continuous measurements of their particle density, in the regime where the bosonization approach is applicable. We show that the phases of these systems are controlled by the competition between measurements, which favor the area-law phase and particle tunneling in and out of the Luttinger liquid, which favors the volume-law phase. The volume-law phase in this system is always charge fuzzy. We show that within this framework, the phase boundary between the two phases is given by a universal dependence of the Luttinger parameter on the measurement rate. This behavior can be contrasted with the non-universal phase boundaries in more generic highly excited one dimensional systems whose behavior is no longer captured by the Luttinger liquid framework.

</details>


### [57] [Non-adiabatic Optimisation via Localisation-Delocalisation Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30301)

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典优化问题常可映射为自旋玻璃基态求解，而自旋玻璃是多体局域化研究的典范模型。作者希望借助量子统计力学（特别是 MBL 相变）的直觉来指导新型量子优化算法的构造，以加速并改善量子退火算法的性能，使其更适用于近期量子设备。

Method: 作者研究跨越多体局域化相变的连续反向量子退火，通过考察系统淬火过程中 Landau-Zener 交叉密度来加速算法；用有效 Fokker-Planck 方程描述算法成功概率的演化；将连续动力学 Trotter 化为步数随系统规模增长保持适度的量子电路（FRIO 算法），并给出确定含时哈密顿量参数的定量策略；最终在 SK 模型上与预优化角度的 QAOA 进行数值对比。

Result: 对具有非零能量密度的参考态，所需退火时间随系统尺寸 N 的标度从 O(poly(N)) 改善为 O(1)；连续动力学可 Trotter 化，且所需步数随系统尺寸增大保持适度；数字电路实现的数值结果表明，在利用热化动力学高效选定电路参数后，FRIO 在求解 SK 自旋玻璃基态问题上几乎总是优于预优化角度的传统 QAOA。

Conclusion: 借助 MBL 相变与 Landau-Zener 交叉密度的物理直觉，可以构造出性能更强的量子优化算法 FRIO。其退火时间对系统尺寸呈现 O(1) 标度，且能被适度步数的 Trotter 化电路实现，在 SK 模型上优于预优化角度的 QAOA，显示出对近期量子设备有较大吸引力。

Abstract: The promise of efficiently solving classical optimisation problems on quantum computers is tantalizing. As many such problems can be mapped to approximating the ground state of spin-glasses, paradigmatic models for studying many-body localisation, it is natural to ask if some of the intuition coming from quantum statistical mechanics can help guide the construction of novel quantum optimisation algorithms. In this work, we discuss how continuous reverse quantum annealing crossing a many-body localisation transition can be accelerated by considering the density of Landau-Zener crossings as the system is quenched. We provide an interpretation of this behaviour in terms of an effective Fokker-Planck equation for the success probability of the algorithm. This interpretation suggests an improvement in the required annealing time as a function of the system size $N$, which goes from $\mathcal{O}({\rm poly}(N))$ to $\mathcal{O}(1)$ for reference states with non-vanishing energy density. We show that the continuous dynamics can be Trotterised with a number of steps that remains modest as system size increases, making it appealing for near-term devices. For the resulting circuit implementation, we provide a quantitative strategy for determining the parameters of the time-dependent Hamiltonian. We compare the performance of our digitised algorithm, which we call FRIO, against that of the QAOA for finding the ground state of spin-glass problems, in particular the SK model. The numerical results suggest that with suitably selected circuit parameters which can be efficiently found using thermalizing dynamics, FRIO almost always outperforms the conventional QAOA ansatz with pre-optimized angles.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 通过解决局域化学环境与扩展周期边界之间的冲突，该框架为复杂凝聚态体系中短程有序的解读提供了物理透明且高效的新范式，在无序材料建模中具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Modeling short-range interactions in highly disordered materials remains challenging due to the immense configuration space and the disruption of local symmetry in truncated theoretical models. Here, we propose a symmetry-preserving local representation framework that strictly enforces topological continuity and translational invariance via boundary passivation. Applied to high-entropy alloys, this approach autonomously unravels the nonlinear ligand effects modulating local reactivity and accurately captures coverage-dependent many-body interactions without explicit periodic expansion. By resolving the conflict between localized chemical environments and extended periodic boundaries, this framework not only reduces computational complexity by an order of magnitude but also provides a physically transparent paradigm for deciphering short-range order in complex condensed matter systems.

</details>

*Arsineh Apelian,Tanay Tak,Xuance Jiang,Tsung-Yin Tsai,James S. Speck,Vojtech Vlcek*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 无序可使价带与导带态发生不同程度的局域化，但局域化对比度的可观测后果此前尚不清楚。本文以In_xGa_{1-x}N随机合金为例，证明局域化对比度强烈影响竞争性的载流子复合通道：In含量增加使价带顶态逐渐局域化而导带底态相对离域，从而降低辐射复合的光学动量矩阵元；而俄歇-迈特纳复合的耦合由原子尺度无序破坏平移对称性、改变态的空间与相位结构所决定，其耦合强度相对纯净GaN增强超五个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示价带与导带态局域化程度的对比（localization contrast）是否并如何影响半导体的载流子复合通道，从而弥合辐射复合与俄歇复合在无序系统中行为趋势的理论空白。

Method: 以随机In_xGa_{1-x}N合金为模型体系，通过系统改变In含量（从x=13.5%至x=22%）调控局域化对比度，结合第一性原理与微观理论分析，计算光学动量矩阵元与俄歇-迈特纳（Auger-Meitner）耦合系数，并考察原子尺度无序对动量空间与相位结构的影响。

Result: 增加In含量使价带最大态渐进局域化而导带最小态相对保持离域；这种局域化对比度降低辐射复合的带间光学动量矩阵元。对俄歇-迈特纳复合，局域化本身不决定耦合强度，原子尺度无序打破原胞平移对称性并产生额外低Q分量，使俄歇耦合相对纯净GaN增强超过五个数量级。

Conclusion: 本文为辐射复合与俄歇复合的相反趋势提供了微观解释，并确立“局域化对比度”作为无序调控半导体光-载流子相互作用与载流子-载流子相互作用的一种普遍机制。

Abstract: Disorder can localize valence- and conduction-band states to different degrees, yet whether such localization contrast has observable consequences remains poorly understood. Here, we show that localization contrast strongly impacts competing carrier-recombination channels. We illustrate this behavior in random In$_x$Ga$_{1-x}$N alloys. Increasing the In content from $x=13.5\%$ to $x=22\%$ progressively localizes the valence-band maximum state while leaving the conduction-band minimum state comparatively delocalized. This contrasting localization decreases the interband optical momentum matrix element underlying radiative recombination. For Auger-Meitner recombination, however, localization alone does not determine the coupling; atomic-scale disorder also breaks primitive-cell translational symmetry and modifies the momentum-space and phase structure of the states. This generates additional low-$Q$ components and enhances the Auger coupling by more than five orders of magnitude relative to pristine GaN. Thus, we provide a microscopic explanation for the competing trends in radiative and Auger recombination. More broadly, our results establish localization contrast as a mechanism through which disorder can control optical and carrier-carrier interactions in semiconductors.

</details>

### [60] [Stochastic Diversity Stabilization in Nonlinear Competitive Dynamics: A Statistical Mechanics Approach to Fluctuation Suppression and Finite-Size Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30169)
*Ruocun Ou*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文在带加性高斯噪声的非线性随机复制子动力学中，定量刻画了维持多样性所需的最小噪声强度随系统维度 N 的幂律衰减,并揭示其指数 alpha 是模型依赖量而非普适指标。通过 (gamma, sigma) 平面扫描，发现波动磁化率 chi_H 在 gamma~0.7 处取峰值但无真正二阶相变，且熵弛豫时间 tau_relax 几乎不随 gamma 变化，表明波动抑制源于"通路多样性"而非加速弛豫。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机强迫虽能阻止竞争动力系统多样性的崩溃，但其定量条件与动力学机制长期缺乏清晰刻画。作者意图回答两个核心问题:(1)维持多样性所需最小噪声强度如何随系统规模 N 和模型参数变化;(2)这种噪声诱导稳定化背后的动力学机制究竟是什么。

Method: 作者研究 N 维概率单纯形上的一类非线性随机复制子动力学，以非线性指数 gamma 和强度 sigma 的加性高斯噪声为参数。方法包括:(1)线性稳定性分析,预测临界噪声幂律指数 alpha=(gamma+2)/2;(2)数值扫描 (gamma, sigma) 平面并计算波动磁化率 chi_H=N·Var(H);(3)有限尺寸标度分析,检验 chi_H 峰值是否随 N 发散以判定相变真伪;(4)测量熵弛豫时间 tau_relax(gamma) 并对照 chi_H 的抑制行为。

Result: 在基准相互作用下，维持多样性的最小噪声随维度按 sigma_c~N^(-alpha) 衰减，alpha~1.1；改用超线性相互作用后 alpha 增至约 2.0，与线性稳定预测 alpha=(gamma+2)/2 吻合，表明 alpha 是模型依赖量。chi_H 在 gamma~0.7 处出现峰值(约 0.6)，在高 gamma 下被抑制，且在 gamma~1.4 附近无发散；有限尺寸分析未发现峰值随 N 的系统性发散，排除真正二阶相变。tau_relax(gamma) 在整个 gamma 范围内大致恒定(约 2.9 个时间单位)，而 chi_H 却下降六倍，显示弛豫时间与波动幅度相互解耦。

Conclusion: 这类非平衡随机系统的主导稳定化模式是"经由通路多样性的非线性波动抑制"——即不同演化路径趋于相似的终点，而非沿单一通道加速弛豫。该机制既区别于噪声诱导相变，也区别于平衡态临界性，为本类系统提供了新的稳定化解释框架。

Abstract: Stochastic forcing can prevent diversity collapse in competitive dynamical systems, yet the quantitative conditions and the dynamical mechanism responsible remain poorly characterized. We address both questions in a class of nonlinear stochastic replicator dynamics on the N-dimensional probability simplex, parameterized by a nonlinearity exponent gamma and additive Gaussian noise of intensity sigma. Under the baseline interaction, the minimum noise required to sustain diversity decays as a power law, sigma_c ~ N^(-alpha); replacing the baseline interaction with a superlinear variant shifts alpha from about 1.1 to about 2.0, in agreement with a linear-stability prediction alpha = (gamma+2)/2, identifying alpha as a model-specific quantity rather than a universal index. Scanning the (gamma, sigma) plane reveals a smooth dynamical crossover: the fluctuation susceptibility chi_H = N Var(H) peaks at gamma ~ 0.7 (chi_H ~ 0.6) and is suppressed at high gamma, with no divergence near gamma ~ 1.4; matched finite-size analysis finds no systematic divergence of the peak with N, ruling out a genuine second-order phase transition. The entropy relaxation time tau_relax(gamma) remains approximately constant (about 2.9 time units) across the full range of gamma, even as chi_H decreases sixfold; this decoupling between relaxation timescale and fluctuation amplitude implies that suppression arises from convergence of diverse pathways to similar endpoints rather than from accelerated relaxation along a single channel. These findings identify nonlinear fluctuation suppression via pathway multiplicity -- distinct from both noise-induced transitions and equilibrium criticality -- as the operative stabilization pattern in this class of nonequilibrium stochastic systems.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 孤立对模拟验证了参考周期并解析出有限耦合修正。链在同一时间尺度上达到第一极小值，极小值更浅且伴随较小的后期残余。与混沌胞和能量守恒噪声的比较显示，在测量窗口内相干回传会出现缺失或被中断的区间。

Conclusion: 这些有限时间结果为区分早期交换平台与后续关联动力学提供了定量参照，可用于热导率推断中识别并剥离局域交换贡献。

Abstract: Distinguishing rapid local energy exchange from sustained transport matters when thermal conductivity is inferred from finite simulations. In a weakly coupled chain of separable quartic oscillators, we identify a quantitative mechanism that changes an early Green--Kubo plateau: resonant neighbors exchange energy and coherently return it. The pair heat-current integral maps to the classical pendulum trapping response. Quartic homogeneity gives an energy-independent reference period $T_{\rm lib}=7.77ε^{-1/2}$, where $ε$ is the coupling. Without fitted parameters, the leading response predicts a negative first minimum at $0.952T_{\rm lib}$ with depth $-0.74$ times the independent-cell exchange coefficient. Isolated-pair simulations test this reference and resolve finite-coupling corrections. The chain reaches its first minimum on the same time scale, with a shallower minimum and a small later residual. Comparisons with chaotic cells and energy-conserving noise show regimes in which coherent return is absent or interrupted over the measured windows. These finite-time results provide a quantitative reference for separating the early exchange plateau from subsequent correlated dynamics.

</details>


### [62] [Foresight-dependent entropy production in stochastic two-agent dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30189)
*Arpan Dey*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究在互惠协调偏好但带前瞻性优化的随机线性二次博弈中，非互易交互如何通过稳态概率流打破细致平衡，并精确计算熵产生率及其对规划时标τ的标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易交互通常依靠维持稳态概率流来打破细致平衡（即产生统计时间不可逆性）。此前通常默认不可逆性源于即时协调偏好的非互易性，但作者追问：当智能体的即时协调偏好是完全互惠的、而其状态调节规则却由前瞻性优化（远见）决定时，是否依然能涌现稳态概率流？这一问题旨在厘清"远见/前瞻优化"本身能否作为统计不可逆性的独立来源。

Method: 作者构建了一个最小的随机线性二次（LQ）博弈：两个智能体控制带噪声的标量状态，在全信息反馈纳什均衡下最小化各自贴现成本。优化后的控制量给出一个 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 过程，其稳态概率流与熵产生率（度量统计时间不可逆性）可精确求解。针对互惠协调但自锚定强度不等的情形，作者通过耦合的纳什 Riccati 方程推导出有效漂移不对称在 τ^5 阶才首次出现、归一化熵产生按 τ^8 标度的抵消机制；对随众-逆众（conformist-contrarian）耦合给出短视极限与 τ^2 修正，并对等自锚定强度的随众-逆众对确定长视极限；最后用纳什方程数值解验证渐近律。

Result: 对互惠协调且自锚定强度不等的情形，有效漂移不对称首次出现在 τ^5 阶，归一化熵产生在 τ→0 极限下按 τ^8 标度，该抵消由耦合纳什 Riccati 方程导出。对随众-逆众耦合，归一化熵产生在短视极限下趋于非零值，并先按 τ^2 量级从该极限下降。对等自锚定强度的随众-逆众对，进一步确定了长视极限。数值求解完整纳什方程验证了这些渐近律并揭示了远见效应。

Conclusion: 前瞻性（远见）优化本身可以生成或降低归一化稳态不可逆性，具体取决于底层交互类型（互惠协调 vs. 随众-逆众耦合）。这证明即使即时协调偏好互惠，远见驱动的状态调节也能打破细致平衡、产生稳态概率流，从而扩展了对非平衡稳态来源的理解。

Abstract: Nonreciprocal interactions can break detailed balance by sustaining stationary probability currents. Here we ask whether such currents can also emerge when the agents' immediate coordination preferences are reciprocal, but their state-adjustment rules are determined by forward-looking optimization (foresight). We study a minimal stochastic linear-quadratic game in which two agents control noisy scalar states and minimize individual discounted costs in a full-information feedback Nash equilibrium. The optimized controls yield an Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process whose stationary probability current and entropy-production rate, measuring statistical time-irreversibility, can be calculated exactly. For reciprocal coordination with unequal self-anchoring strengths, the effective drift asymmetry first appears at order $τ^5$, where $τ$ is the planning timescale, and the normalized entropy production consequently scales as $τ^8$ in the limit $τ\to0$. We derive this cancellation from the coupled Nash Riccati equations. For conformist-contrarian coupling, normalized entropy production approaches a nonzero short-foresight limit and initially decreases from that limit by an amount proportional to $τ^2$. We also determine the long-foresight limit for a conformist-contrarian pair with equal self-anchoring strengths. Numerical solutions of the full Nash equations verify the asymptotic laws and reveal the effect of foresight. Thus, forward-looking optimization can generate or reduce normalized stationary irreversibility, depending on the underlying interaction.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文探讨了爱因斯坦扩散理论中"正常非Fickian扩散"的起源，指出空间依赖的扩散系数可以导致非Fickian扩散，而这种扩散仍属于正常扩散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史上爱因斯坦在推导扩散理论时假设了均匀扩散（扩散常数为常数），得出Fickian扩散。但同样的推导中隐含着向空间依赖扩散系数情形（非Fickian扩散）推广的种子。作者旨在阐明这种非Fickian扩散如何能仍属正常扩散，并澄清其理论起源。

Method: 通过重新审视爱因斯坦最初的扩散理论推导，揭示其中蕴含的空间依赖扩散系数的推广可能性，并讨论正常非Fickian扩散的理论来源。

Result: 非Fickian扩散（可驱动浓度不均匀性）也可以属于正常扩散，其根源可追溯至爱因斯坦原始推导中所隐藏的空间依赖扩散系数的推广。

Conclusion: 正常扩散并不局限于Fickian（均匀）扩散；空间依赖扩散系数所导致的非Fickian扩散同样属于正常扩散，且其理论源头已蕴含于爱因斯坦最初的推导之中。

Abstract: Einstein's theory of diffusion defines what is considered {\it normal diffusion}. Historically, Einstein considered homogeneous diffusion characterised by a diffusion constant in his original calculations, resulting in what is known as {\it Fickian} diffusion, which thermally relaxes concentration inhomogeneities. However, in the same derivation of Einstein, the seed of generalisation of the theory to {\it non-Fickian} diffusion in the presence of space-dependent diffusivity is hidden. This indicates that non-Fickian diffusion, which can drive concentration inhomogeneities, can also be normal diffusion. The present article discusses the origin of the {\it normal} non-Fickian diffusion in Einstein's theory of diffusion.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate a hierarchy of invariant Hilbert-space structures connecting Hilbert-space fragmentation, partial solvability, and quantum many-body scars. For spin chains with fragmented spin-$1/2$ subspaces and their local deformations, we show, using the spin-$1$ $XXC$ chain as a representative example, how successive local deformations reduce extensive irreducible-string fragmentation through an implicitly embedded spin-$1/2$ invariant space and ultimately to a single scar tower, connecting the $XXC$, perturbed $XXC$, and spin-$1$ $XY$ scar models. Through the intermediate embedded space, local dynamics couples states from distinct frozen irreducible-string sectors and selects a new decomposition into representations, from which smaller invariant spaces can be retained. We also establish a rigidity constraint on isolating nonsymmetric towers and a constructive correspondence that transfers a reduction between locally realizable representations of the embedded dynamics. The latter demonstrates that the reduction hierarchy can be transferred through a unitary transformation within the embedded subspace, yielding an exact scar tower built on a Néel reference state without requiring unitary equivalence of the full Hamiltonians.

Abstract: Inferring effective equations from observations of nonlinear stochastic processes is challenging because individual trajectories are noisy realizations, while the relevant physical information is encoded in ensemble statistics. We introduce a data-driven framework that learns a change of variables in which nonlinear stochastic dynamics become linear. The model combines a neural change of representation with a linear stochastic latent evolution and is trained through a short-time moment-matching objective, thereby avoiding trajectory \rev{simulation} during optimization. This construction yields an interpretable generative model: both the learned transformation and the latent evolution operators can be directly inspected.

From simulated height trajectories alone, the model recovers the local exponential Cole--Hopf map and the associated linear stochastic operator. Generated trajectories reproduce the main statistical signatures of the KPZ universality class, including the growth law $\mathrm{Var}(h^{t})\sim t^{2β}$ with $β=1/3$ and the GOE Tracy--Widom height distribution for flat initial conditions. These results show that interpretable stochastic equations can be inferred from data by learning representations in which the dynamics closes in a simple form. Furthermore the development of the physical informed learning permits to get insights on the physics of the dynamical process.

</details>

Abstract: Active phase separation in living systems is ubiquitous, yet the microscopic origins of the non-equilibrium terms in active field theories remain largely unknown. Here, starting from stochastic mass-conserving reaction-diffusion dynamics of two interacting chemical species, we perform a systematic slow-mode reduction and derive the complete Active Model B+ field theory, with all phenomenological coefficients expressed in terms of microscopic parameters. We identify concentration-dependent diffusivities and asymmetric inter-species chemotactic couplings as the minimal microscopic ingredients generating the genuinely non-equilibrium coefficient responsible for microphase separation, cluster phases, and arrested coarsening. Abstract: Improving the efficiency of a stochastic search often requires to provide the searching agent with resources that modify its dynamics and help it explore space more quickly. Although such resources are often assumed to be unlimited and treated as a cost to mitigate, many realistic search processes operate under a finite resource budget, e.g. in processes with environmental memory in which the searcher deposits long-lasting cues along its path that it can interact with at later times to guide its further search. Here, we address the question of how to allocate a limited budget to maximize search efficiency by investigating a one-dimensional diffusing agent whose access to resources is constrained. We consider three resource models, namely an external force applied by an external controller to pull the searcher towards the target, directional flags deposited by the searcher along its path that redirect its motion towards the target, and self-generated chemorepulsive cues that act as a force field. For all these models, we find that resource usage should be concentrated in narrow regions of space when information about the searcher's current and past positions is available. In contrast, in blind strategies where a predefined time protocol is followed, resources should be used at a steady rate to ensure it to be used at most of its capacity by the time it reaches the target. We further show that blind strategies in non-Markovian processes with environmental memory are significantly less efficient due to the inherent fluctuations in the locations of deposited cues.

</details>

Abstract: We consider boundary conditions of two-dimensional Dirac fermions which preserve a non-anomalous continuous symmetry which acts differently on the left- and right-movers. Such boundaries are equivalent to topological defects of Weyl fermions preserving the symmetry. We give a general construction of such defects for conformally-embedded symmetries using Chern-Simons theory. They always exist and can be realised as half-space gauging defects for some finite categorical symmetry. Using this technology, we compute the twist lines to which fermions become attached upon scattering through the defect, the boundary central charge of the corresponding folded boundary state, and its mod-2 valued SPT class. As applications, we first consider abelian symmetries and find agreement with known results in the literature. We then study a novel example where an $SU(2) \times SU(4) \times U(1)^2$ symmetry is preserved. In contrast to the abelian case, the twist lines are intrinsically non-invertible and belong to either a $\text{TY}(\mathbb{Z}_3 \times \mathbb{Z}_3)$ or $\text{Rep}(\mathbb{S}_3) \times \mathbb{Z}_3$ symmetry depending on whether scattering is to the left or the right.

Abstract: Holographic complexity admits a broad family of bulk observables, yet their relation to boundary state-preparation costs remains unclear. We characterise the resulting preparation penalties relative to the Fubini-Study metric and compute them for the maximal volume in scalar perturbations of AdS$_3$. At large-conformal dimension, we recover the known proportionality between cost and Fubini-Study metrics. At finite dimension, we obtain positive but generally unequal penalties for source variations that displace the bulk field and its conjugate momentum, breaking this proportionality even for a single scalar operator. These additions selectively change field-displacement costs, with the gradient term introducing mode dependence. Our results provide an explicit local connection between the choice of bulk observables and the assignment of boundary preparation penalties.

Abstract: The Euclidean saddles of 3D Einstein gravity with positive cosmological constant are spherical three-manifolds. This leads to a spherical volume conjecture for the product of continued Virasoro TQFT amplitudes. As further evidence, we relate the theta and tetrahedron graph amplitudes to the two-point function and out-of-time-ordered four-point function of operators on a massive observer's worldline. In addition, we analyze examples with knotted worldlines and reproduce their spherical volumes. We also apply our prescription to Seifert manifolds, which are off-shell in AdS$_3$ gravity but admit dS$_3$ saddles, and find agreement with their spherical volumes semiclassically. Finally, using Dehn surgery on torus knots and links, we obtain partition functions on smooth spherical three-manifolds.

The relevant deformations identified in our calculations break at least one preserved symmetry, providing evidence that these monodromy defects are stable against symmetry-preserving relevant perturbations. On a randomly selected test set, selecting the top-ranked $5\%$ recovers $47.2\%$ of the SUSY test solutions. We also use supervised regression to predict the potential and moduli field values. Abstract: Seven-dimensional supergravity poses a uniquely intricate supersymmetry problem, combining topologically massive higher-form gauge dynamics, distinct Clifford algebra properties, and rich geometric structures. We first establish the complete formulation of the maximal Cartan-gauged theory in terms of the gauge-invariant higher-form flux, including both the bosonic action and supersymmetry variations. This formulation, which also makes the smooth ungauged limit manifest, is achieved by requiring a consistent truncation to the minimal Cartan-gauged theory in the equal-charge limit. We construct the complete bilinear system for a timelike Killing vector, where additional internal orientation of the symplectic-Majorana spinor arises already in the scalar sector, uniquely among pure minimal ungauged theories. In the equal-charge sector, we decouple the algebraic constraints into SU$(3)$-isotypic components, and analyse the differential bilinear relations, determining the permitted dilaton gradient, fluxes, fibration twist and intrinsic torsion of the base. We prove that with gauging and active Cartan flux, the complex scalar bilinear vanishes identically. We also construct a Killing gauge for the higher-form potential compatible with the self-duality condition. We show that with gauging but no active Cartan flux, the Killing vector also acts as a generator of the gauged R-symmetry, and the base structure is enlarged from SU$(3)$ to U$(3)$. We find that BPS-flow solutions realize this setting. With supersymmetry, we isolate a single independent component of the 3-form equation across an exhaustive four-class partition for both theories within this setup. Applied to known and proposed solutions in both theories, this classification exposes unexplored sectors and provides an accessible toolbox for constructing and testing solutions and developing new geometric and holographic applications.

Abstract: The same leading $T\bar T$ interaction can coexist with sharply different strong-field responses, even within causal, classically integrable sigma models. We reconstruct exact autonomous stress-tensor generators for prescribed one-scale Courant--Hilbert families in two dimensions. Separating the invariant stress scale from a signed trace ratio reduces reconstruction to one scalar inversion on each invertible physical branch. The resulting profile determines both the deformation law and the relation between local energy density and pressure. For generalized Born--Infeld and logarithmic models, the leading interactions coincide, yet pressure saturates in the former and grows without bound in the latter. Their exact generators are respectively quadratic and nonpolynomial, the latter involving Lambert $W_{-1}$. A regular Legendre transformation gives the same profile in the auxiliary potential formulation. The flows commute locally with root-$T\bar T$ within a common smooth branch and retain a common classical Lax form. Exact reconstruction thus quantifies the nonlinear freedom left by leading-order matching.

</details>

Conclusion: 例外点处必须对合并的模式整体处理，EFT 修正的普通单频率展开不再适用；此现象对全响应函数及其他黑洞物理领域具有重要启示。

Abstract: We show that the ordinary expansion of EFT corrections to individual QNM frequencies breaks down at an exceptional QNM, and that these corrections become enhanced for near-exceptional QNMs. We give a brief argument for why this should generally happen, before numerically demonstrating this behaviour by calculating the $α'^3$ corrections to QNMs of equal-spin Myers--Perry-AdS$_5$ black holes. This calculation corresponds to $λ^{-3/2}$ corrections to the poles of the retarded Green's function in the boundary CFT, where $λ$ is the 't Hooft coupling. Treating the coalescing pair together, we calculate the leading $λ^{-3/4}$ splitting at the uncorrected exceptional point and the $λ^{-3/2}$ displacement of the exceptional point in temperature and angular velocity. The background derivative expansion can remain valid while the nondegenerate expansion of individual frequencies fails. We discuss the implications for the full response and for other areas of black hole physics.

</details>

Abstract: The S matrix on an asymptotically-flat Schwarzschild black hole is studied as a function of position on $\mathcal{I}$. We derive the lightcone singularities that arise in the S matrix whenever the two points on $\mathcal{I}$ are connected by a bulk null geodesic. This includes geodesics orbiting near the photon sphere for a long time, whose kinematics determine the associated lightcone singularities. By turning on a finite string length $\ell_s$, worldsheet corrections are shown to shift the lightcone singularities into the complex time plane, resolving the singularities at real times. In particular, we derive the leading stringy resolution of the photon sphere singularities. We also compute stringy corrections to the eikonal quasinormal mode spectrum of Schwarzschild, and discuss the kinematics of strings orbiting the photon sphere.

</details>

Abstract: We construct a family of integrable 2d sigma models through 4d Chern-Simons theory and show that their classical point-particle reduction corresponds to a Hitchin system. The integrable Hitchin quantisation then provides a natural candidate Hamiltonian for the one-loop reduced dynamics of the sigma models, which coincides with the Perelman operator in all cases examined. This allows us to study the corresponding spectral problems using 4d $\mathcal{N}=2$ gauge theory and 2d conformal field theory methods. For the sausage model, we derive exact quantisation conditions and compute exponentially small corrections essential for the agreement with numerical finite-volume results.

Abstract: We develop a new framework for evaluating higher-loop integrals for scalar fields on $S^D$. Starting from a Schwinger-parametric form for general higher-loop integrals on the sphere, it combines total-derivative identities with edge-contraction relations into a coupled linear system. To illustrate our method, we solve for all vacuum graphs through two loops. This includes, in particular, the two-loop three-melon, for which we construct the analytic solution for general $D$ and obtain explicit dimensionally regularized results at all integer dimensions.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在伴随论文中已对这族 Narain CFT 的偶自对偶粘合码做了分类（二十二个码、七个不等价理论），本文旨在给出该族的物理解释，并利用三维拓扑场论理解这些边界的结构、对称性组织方式以及理论间的关联（如平均化）与 Siegel-Weil 恒等式。

Abstract: In a companion paper arXiv:2610.26610, we classified the Narain conformal field theories obtained by gluing copies of the $A_3$ root and weight lattices along their $\mathbb{Z}_4$ discriminant group. For two hyperbolic pairs, this gives twenty-two even self-dual gluing codes, which fall into seven inequivalent theories at $c=\bar c=6$. In this paper, we develop the physical interpretation of this family and its connections to three-dimensional topological field theory. We show that all twenty-two codes are topological boundary conditions of a single abelian Chern-Simons theory. Its invertible symmetries, which include fractional $B$-field shifts, organize the boundaries into three orbits: free, mixed and torsion. Boundaries in different orbits are related only by gauging. We determine all gaugings of order at most four between the seven theories, by $\mathbb{Z}_2$, $\mathbb{Z}_4$ and $\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$, with explicit twisted partition functions, and follow their effect on the spectrum and the current algebra. We then sum the Chern-Simons partition function over handlebodies at genus one and prove a finite Siegel-Weil identity, valid for any number of pairs. For two pairs, the handlebody sum is the uniform average over the free theories. Recovering the average over all twenty-two theories requires one additional contribution, a solid torus with the torsion lines along its core. For three or more pairs, we find that the handlebody sum is not a positive combination of code boundary states.

</details>


### [94] [Beyond the Fock Vacuum in Finite-$N$ Bilocal Algebras of Vector Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30572)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双局域全息的矢量模型通常只研究建立在Fock真空之上的扇区，但完整的有限N希尔伯特空间还包含由保色群对偶作用（Howe对偶）带来的其他扇区。作者希望系统地找出并刻画这些超出真空的扇区，理解它们与色群阿贝尔化及其特征的关系，并给出这些新扇区中物理态的精确有限N描述。

Result: 得出O(N)给出两个扇区、U(N)给出整数行列式荷扇区、Sp(N)因是完美群不给额外扇区；统计性质与模式数决定扇区是否实际实现。两个玻色O(N)扇区不能被任何Casimir区分。显式得到O(N)与U(N)的sector分解特征标；新背景附近以辅助振子求解约束，玻色物质构成Fermi海。在R×S^2自由矢量模型中，新扇区由重子型原初算符组成，并精确给出其有限N下的能量、简并度、自旋内容以及固定角动量下的最低能量。

Conclusion: Howe对偶提供了超出Fock真空的完整有限N双局域扇区的统一框架：这些额外扇区由色群G的阿贝尔化及其特征所控制（O(N)两扇区、U(N)行列式荷扇区、Sp(N)无额外扇区），并通过epsilon/determinant背景显式构造。该方法给出了有限N下重子型原初算符的精确谱数据，为双局域全息中非真空扇区的完整描述奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: We discuss the finite-$N$ Hilbert space of bilocal operators that underlies bilocal holography of vector models, beyond the sector built on the Fock vacuum. If the microscopic oscillator Fock space carries a multiplicity-free dual action of a compact color group $G$ and a bilocal algebra, Howe duality implies that projection to the derived subgroup $[G,G]$ selects precisely the characters of the abelianization $G^{\rm ab}=G/[G,G]$ that occur in the Fock space. Thus $O(N)/SO(N)=\mathbb Z_2$ gives two sectors, $U(N)/SU(N)=U(1)$ gives integer determinant-charge sectors, while $Sp(N)$ is perfect and gives no additional sector; Bose/Fermi statistics and the number of modes determine which sectors are actually realized. We construct the epsilon and determinant backgrounds that generate these sectors, together with their finite-$N$ constraints and Casimirs, and find that the two bosonic $O(N)$ sectors are not distinguished by any Casimir. Using group-integral projectors, we derive explicit sector-resolved characters for the $O(N)$ and $U(N)$ theories. Around each new background we solve the constraints with auxiliary oscillators, which form a Fermi sea for bosonic matter. In the free vector model on $\mathbb R\times S^2$ the new sectors consist of baryonic primaries; we determine their energies, degeneracies and spin content exactly at finite $N$, together with their lowest energy at fixed angular momentum.

</details>

*Ioanna Kourkoulou,David Tong*

TL;DR: 本文研究四维时空中自由格点费米子在 staggered、Wilson 和 overlap 三种离散化方案下的精确 Onsager 代数，证明其在低能极限下还原出 U(2N) 手征对称性，并讨论了生成元量子化/超局域性差异及局域性与紧子群之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 格点费米子正则化通常会破坏连续理论中的手征对称性，作者希望揭示精确格点 Onsager 代数与低能手征对称性之间的联系，从而理解不同离散化方案如何隐藏或涌现手征对称性。

Method: 在四维时空中，分别采用哈密顿表述和欧几里得表述，对 staggered、Wilson 和 overlap 三种自由格点费米子构造其精确 Onsager 代数；分析各方案中生成元的量子化性与超局域性，并考察 Onsager 代数的最大紧子群及其与生成元局域性的关系。

Result: 在每种情形下，精确格点代数均涌现出低能理论的完整手征对称性：若连续极限中有 N 个无质量狄拉克费米子，Onsager 代数约化为 U(2N) 手征对称。哪些生成元被量子化或呈超局域在不同表述之间存在差异，且生成元的局域性与 Onsager 代数的最大紧子群之间存在权衡关系。

Conclusion: 格点 Onsager 代数为理解格点手征对称性提供了统一框架，不同费米子离散化方案在对称性实现机制上虽有细节差异，但都在低能极限下恢复完整的 U(2N) 手征对称性。

Abstract: We study the exact Onsager algebra of free lattice fermions in staggered, Wilson, and overlap incarnations, working in both Hamiltonian and Euclidean formulations in four spacetime dimensions. In each case, we show that the exact lattice algebra emanates the full chiral symmetry of the low-energy theory: if there are $N$ massless Dirac fermions in the continuum, the Onsager algebra reduces to a $U(2N)$ chiral symmetry. The details of which generators are quantised or ultra-local differ between formulations. There is also a trade-off between the locality of generators and the maximum compact subgroup of the Onsager algebra.

</details>


### [96] [A periodic $\bar q$ equation for form factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30633)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 周期 $\bar q$ 方程成立，为形状因子提供了类似振幅 $\bar Q$ 方程的递推框架；其共线积分的结构给出与已知结果一致的全圈MHV最后条目预言，并与周期威尔逊圈图景下的全圈推导相容，进一步经形状因子OPE验证了费米子能量的一圈修正。

Abstract: MHV form factors of the chiral stress-tensor multiplet in planar $\mathcal N=4$ super Yang-Mills theory are dual to periodic null Wilson loops. We propose a periodic $\bar q$ equation for the MHV and higher-helicity sectors at vanishing fermionic monodromy. The equation relates kinematic derivatives of $\mathrm N^k\mathrm{MHV}$ form factors to collinear integrals of $\mathrm N^{k+1}\mathrm{MHV}$ form factors with one additional external particle, providing a recursive relation in loop order. In periodic momentum supertwistors, it has the same collinear-integral form as the two dotted components of the amplitude $\bar Q$ equation, with coefficient $Γ_{\rm cusp}/4$, despite the absence of ordinary dual conformal invariance at fixed operator momentum. For parity-even scalar MHV remainders, combining the equation with its parity image supplies all directions of the kinematic differential.
  We verify the equation analytically for the one-loop four-point NMHV form factor and reconstruct the two-loop three-point MHV remainder at function level. Further two-loop MHV tests give numerical agreement with derivatives of the known four-point remainder at a fixed kinematic configuration, and for five points, all six chiral derivatives agree exactly at symbol level for a one-parameter family of kinematics. The structure of the collinear source further gives all-loop predictions for MHV last entries, consistent with known results and form a space of dimension $n(2n-5)$. The periodic Wilson-loop picture also motivates an all-loop derivation. Using the form-factor OPE, we extract the one-loop correction to the fermion energy from the logarithmic source and recover its vanishing at zero momentum.

</details>

Method: 构造一个具有(0,2)超对称的扭旋线性σ模型，其大半径低能极限给出五维杂化弦flux真空；利用该线性σ模型作为桥梁，连接到大半径几何与由Gepner模型和圆构成并通过非对称轨道折叠得到的弦论区描述；同时发展了一系列适用于更一般(0,2)规范线性σ模型（gauged linear sigma model, GLSM）构造杂化真空的工具。

Result: 成功实现了一个五维杂化flux真空的扭旋(0,2)线性σ模型构造；证明其大半径相与Gepner模型加圆上的非对称轨道折叠所描述的弦论区相连接，从而为同一真空提供了几何与弦论两套等价描述。

Abstract: We present a torsional linear sigma model with (0,2) supersymmetry that realizes, in its large radius phase, a five-dimensional heterotic flux vacuum, and we use the linear sigma model to connect this geometry to a stringy regime realized as an asymmetric orbifold of a Gepner model and a circle. Along the way we develop a number of tools relevant for more general (0,2) gauged linear sigma model constructions of heterotic vacua.

</details>


### [98] [Quasinormal modes, complex null geodesics and singularity resolution in regular AdS black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30915)
*Heng-Yu Chen,Yasuaki Hikida,Yasutaka Koga*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过正则AdS黑洞研究黑洞奇点分辨如何在共形场论对偶中被编码：WKB分析计算准正规模式并读出两点函数，发现奇异黑洞中弹跳零测地线对应的奇点被光滑凸起取代，源于复而非实的零测地线，其位置由算符乘积展开结构支持，从而给出奇点分辨的全息信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞奇点被视界隐藏，难以判断其是否被量子引力或弦论效应分辨；已知AdS-Schwarzschild黑洞对偶CFT的两点函数存在与弹跳奇点的零测地线相关的奇点，作者想探究奇点分辨如何在对偶CFT中体现。

Method: 以正则AdS黑洞作为正则化几何，利用WKB分析计算其准正规模式，并从模式中读出对偶两点函数；进一步通过两点函数的算符乘积展开结构分析复零测地线奇点的位置。

Result: 奇异黑洞中与弹跳零测地线对应的奇点被一个光滑凸起取代，该凸起关联于复且非实的零测地线；复测地线奇点的位置由OPE结构分析得到支持。

Conclusion: 结果提供了黑洞奇点分辨的全息信号，并阐明了正则内部信息如何被编码在边界关联函数中。

Abstract: The black hole singularity is hidden behind its horizon, and it is difficult to determine whether it is resolved by quantum gravity or stringy effects. For an AdS-Schwarzschild black hole, it is known that the two-point function of the dual conformal field theory has a singularity associated with a null geodesic that bounces off the black hole singularity. In this paper, we investigate how the resolution of the black hole singularity is encoded in the dual conformal field theory by considering regular AdS black holes as regularized geometries. Using WKB analysis, we compute the quasinormal modes of regular AdS black holes and read off the dual two-point functions from the modes. We find that the singularity corresponding to the bouncing null geodesic for singular black holes is replaced by a smooth bump associated with a complex, rather than real, null geodesic. The location of the complex geodesic singularity is supported by analyzing the structure of the operator product expansion for the two-point function. Our results provide a holographic signature of black hole singularity resolution and clarify how information about the regular interior is encoded in boundary correlation functions.

</details>


### [99] [Five-loop static two-body potential in massless scalar-tensor gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30972)
*Gabriel Luz Almeida,Zhengwen Liu,Jia Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在无质量标量-张量理论中计算了致密双体保守势的静态贡献至五阶后牛顿(5PN)阶，通过后牛顿有效场论(EFT)完成1PN-5PN所需的1至5圈无质量两点费曼积分；结果复现到2PN，给出3PN的独立EFT确定，并首次延展到4PN和5PN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为日益精确的引力波检验提供超越广义相对论的引力理论的高精度理论模板，需将标量-张量理论中的双体保守势推进到更高后牛顿阶。

Method: 在无质量标量-张量理论中采用后牛顿有效场论(EFT)，以依赖标量的质量编码致密体的强场标量响应。计算1PN-5PN静态项需进行1至5圈无质量两点费曼积分。尽管附加标量改变了源耦合和被积分子，所需的标量主拓扑仍与广义相对论静态问题相同；两个静态标量扇区的独立反射对称性将奇PN阶因式化机制延展到本理论，从而无需真正新的5圈主积分。

Result: 结果复现了已知的至2PN静态相互作用，在3PN给出独立的EFT确定，并把显式势延展到4PN和5PN。验证了广义相对论极限与体交换对称性；调节子极点和残余超越项在完整结果中相互抵消，最终仅留有理系数。

Conclusion: 这些高精度结果为超越广义相对论的引力波检验提供了日益精确的要素。

Abstract: We compute the static contribution to the conservative two-body potential in massless scalar-tensor theories through fifth post-Newtonian (5PN) order, with the strong-field scalar responses of the compact objects encoded in scalar-dependent masses. Within post-Newtonian effective field theory (EFT), evaluating the 1PN-5PN static terms requires one- through five-loop massless two-point Feynman integrals. Although the additional scalar modifies both source couplings and integrand numerators, the required scalar master topologies remain those of the corresponding static problem in general relativity. Moreover, independent reflection symmetries of the two static scalar sectors extend the odd-PN factorization mechanism to the present theory, so that no genuinely new five-loop master integrals are required. Our results reproduce the known static interaction through 2PN, provide an independent EFT determination at 3PN, and extend the explicit potential to 4PN and 5PN. We verify the general-relativity limit and body-exchange symmetry; regulator poles and residual transcendental terms cancel in the complete result, leaving rational coefficients. These high-precision results provide ingredients for increasingly precise gravitational-wave tests of gravity beyond general relativity.

</details>


### [100] [Small black hole in de Sitter as an observer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31027)
*Guanda Lin,Haifeng Tang*
